- **public-02-claude-skills-main-engineering-team-skills-epic-design-references-depth-system-f92af41c**: The 2.5D illusion is built entirely on a **6-level depth model**. Every element on the page belongs to exactly one depth level. Depth controls four automatic properties: parallax speed, blur, scale, and shadow intensity. Together these four signals trick the human visual system into perceiving genuine spatial depth from flat assets.
